# Rust Compiler Cleanup — September 7, 2026

## Scope

Reopened via repo MCP `ticket_reopen` after reading `repo://goals`. The existing AI-optimized Repo goal remains associated with this ticket. The user requires `s` and all plugins to compile without warnings or errors.

The shipping build includes the native WGPU renderer, the browser WGPU renderer (`wasm32-unknown-unknown`), and plugin and extension components (`wasm32-wasip2`, `wasm-dev` profile). Native test compilation is included to catch test-only regressions. The previous ticket did not finish these surfaces; its earlier exceptions are not considered success for this request.

## Method

- Preserve concurrent changes and make no modifying Git operations.
- Capture compiler diagnostics before editing; resolve underlying causes without warning suppression.
- Run builds through Bun/Nx, with two Cargo jobs and an isolated ticket target directory.
- Keep generated diagnostics and build artifacts in `🗑️generated` until verification ends, then remove them.
- Record actual executed checks and counts below; no passing claim before completion.

## Checks

Native workspace all-target compilation baseline started. Results pending.

### Initial Native Workspace Pass Completed

`bun x nx exec --projects=workspace -- cargo check --workspace --all-targets --keep-going -j 2 --message-format=json` exited 101 (Nx exited 1). It recorded 9859 warning diagnostics and 3376 error diagnostics across all native targets. These are diagnostic counts, including repeated library/test messages. Sources were being corrected during this pass, so it is discovery evidence, not final-state verification.

The empty-enum regression emitted the expected unreachable-call diagnostic before the derive fix. Its fixture include path was corrected and `unreachable_code` is now denied at integration-crate scope. An executable regression run is in progress.

The run host now awaits asynchronous blob, plugin router, compatibility, and transaction APIs. Actor turn completion uses the actor kernel result directly and preserves cold-pair and lifecycle receipts. Layout snapshot JSON now uses the existing local ToValue/FromValue codec. All require fresh compiler verification.

### Enum Regression Passed

The `deny_unknown_fields_enums` integration target ran successfully: **15 passed, 0 failed, 0 ignored**, exit 0, and **zero compiler diagnostics** in the captured JSON stream. The new language-neutral empty/unit enum cases matched serde, with the expected `[DEBUG]` runtime evidence.

### DSL Derive Tests

Executed `cargo test -p semio-framework-os-kernel-dsl-derive --lib -j2 --message-format=json -- --nocapture` through `bun x nx exec --projects=workspace`. Result: exit 0, 14 passed, 0 failed, 0 ignored; no compiler diagnostic messages. Covers the schema-first source-authority and mandatory-mutation metadata fixtures after splitting leaf constant evaluations. Large downstream enum compilation remains to be verified.

### Additional Fixes Under Verification

- UI rendering and input-admission helpers used exclusively by existing unit tests now compile only with tests; removed unused private accessors and a never-constructed internal layout fault. Kept active mounted layout and rendering paths.
- Removed duplicate private database-facade reexports; public crate-root vocabulary remains explicitly exported by the package glue.
- Layout export now borrows page data for the snapshot lifetime and handles current close-step outcomes. Mutation text/binary codecs use the repository value/JSON implementations.
- TIFF baseline removal now computes the inverse when StripOffsets existed instead of evaluating an unconditional early return as a function argument. Added regression coverage using the committed before/after fixtures and serde JSON comparison; pending execution.

### Native Core Check

Executed `cargo check -p semio-framework-os-run -p semio-framework-ui -p semio-framework-os-kernel-db --lib --keep-going -j2 --message-format=json` through Bun/Nx with incremental compilation disabled. Exit 0 after 4m55s: 204 warnings, 0 errors across the dependency graph. This confirms native type checking of the runner/API repairs, but is not a warning-free result and does not replace runtime tests.

### Borrowed Slice Codec Regression

Added a neutral slice corpus and serde oracle test. Before the implementation, the test compilation failed with E0277 (`[i64]: ToValue` missing and implicit Sized bound). Added slice encoding, reused it from Vec/array encoders, and relaxed `to_dsl_value` to accept unsized inputs. The subsequent full replication test run executed 256 tests: 254 passed including the new slice test and mutation metadata tests, 2 existing float-format tests failed (`every_finite_f32_round_trips_bit_exactly_and_prints_like_serde_json`, `f32_widens_through_its_own_shortest_lexeme_not_its_bits`). Overall exit 1; this is not a passing suite.

### Aggregate Evaluation Follow-up

Architect still exceeded the constant-evaluation limit in the selected plugin pass. The derive already fully validates each descriptor and source in an independent constant, so the final aggregate check now performs cross-leaf uniqueness and completeness without repeating all per-leaf path scans. The general full-roster validator retains its checks and shares the uniqueness routine. Pending fresh tests and Architect compilation.

### Decimal Float and Metadata Regression Results

Executed protocol-derive-test4 through Bun/Nx: exit 0, replication 256 passed, DSL derive 14 passed, no compiler warnings/errors. The 200,000-pattern float sweep now retains f32 bits and matches serde's decimal values, including halfway rounding. Prior test runs exposed formatting-threshold, parser-rounding and midpoint differences; those runs failed and are not counted as passing.

Removed three unused private DB operations after verifying no source callers: MemWalRangeCopy and its factory, db_io_memory_list_cursor, and database_sync_hello_envelope_credit, in storage/🦀️.rs and sync/🦀️.rs. Their active retained-operation implementations remain in place.

### Validated Aggregate Scope

The new scope regression first failed with E0599 for the missing validate method. Shared root, filename and layout validation now produces an immutable validated scope; per-leaf checks retain descriptor, identity, workspace and exact source-path checks. After correcting the derive expansion assertion for the new method name, validated-scope-after2 passed: replication 256 tests and derive 14 tests, exit 0, no compiler diagnostics. The full native pass is pending.

Native-shipping2 completed with 815 warnings and 50 errors; Architect no longer reported long_running_const_eval. Its scope validation still took several minutes, motivating the shared scope change. Energy library source checking completed without errors. Renderer library checked, while its native executable had two parsing faults in unsafe-block comparisons; those comparisons are now parenthesized for both Unix and Windows. This was not a successful overall build.

### Remaining Unused Parser Closures and Delta Parameters

Removed unused malformed-error closures and named intentionally unused base/payload parameters in direct delta and relation-check implementations.

## Native and WASI Check Progress

Native-shipping3 completed with 539 warnings and 168 errors across the selected packages. Its obsolete synchronous category wrappers were corrected afterward. Native-renderer4 completed successfully with 606 warnings and no errors, including the native binary with native-bin. Wasi-energy completed successfully with 175 warnings and no errors. These are type checks, not completed component/link/runtime verification. The browser renderer check is now running.

## Shared Warning Corrections

The return-source entry collection and request-session-close helper have only test consumers; compile them with their tests. Workflow inverses that derive their result solely from the payload now name the unused base argument explicitly. Surface resize rejection close still takes the retained operation but no longer evaluates an ignored Boolean.

## DAG Mutation Schema Ownership

The initial mutation-vector test build stopped in a concurrently changed kernel codec before compiling DAG (two Send errors); the earlier native check directly demonstrated both missing mutation metadata implementations. Config and presence now have explicit JSON schemas, source-owned descriptors and direct MutationKind leaves. Aggregate metadata is derived from those leaves, and all Rust callers use the new semantic vocabulary. Camera and locale inverses retain only their changed fields. The JSON oracle vectors and codec/inverse laws will be rerun.

### Complete Warning Gate Scope

The new language-neutral scope fixture failed against the old implementation because native renderer coverage was missing. The updated Nx gate passed three target vectors, two rejected-target vectors, and an independent Cargo metadata comparison covering all 59 plugin/extension crates. Native checks enable the renderer binary; browser checks include the renderer. The launch configurations now call the Nx target, including a scope-only validation entry. Full warning-free compilation remains incomplete.

Changed: root script, launch.json, and the Rust warning scope JSON fixture.

### WASI Fleet Repairs

The 59-crate WASI check exposed a browser-scoped RefCell import used by shared map caches and asynchronous extension builder methods with no suspension points. Made the shared import unconditional, restored immediate pure builder operations, and removed their ready-future wrappers. The control extension bundle constructor now follows the synchronous builder contract shared by the other extensions.

### First DAG Runtime Test Result

The complete DAG library test run compiled with zero errors and 128 warnings, then ran 189 tests: 125 passed and 64 failed. The new config/presence mutation vectors passed. Most failures came from the plugin codec expecting hex JSON while both the committed demo and the framework graph codec use the same structured DSL. Replaced the divergent text implementation with the existing canonical graph codec through the artifact-owned conversion boundary. Added a handcrafted JSON oracle for the demo nodes and edges, parsed independently with Serde. Tests must be rerun.

### Aggregate Source Ownership Investigation

The WASI compile reports aggregate-source const failures for Mathematical (15 leaves) and Note (33 leaves). Mathematical explicitly imports graph/geometry/equation mutation leaves from separate owning subsets while its aggregate remains under the any subset. The current Mutations derive constructs only one validated source scope from its own directory, so it rejects those deliberately separated leaves. Draw has the same split but also lacks MutationLeaf metadata on fourteen leaves. A clean fix must explicitly declare the aggregate’s component source roots while preserving exact workspace, taxonomy, source-path, descriptor, semantic identity, and roster uniqueness checks. No ownership validation has been weakened and no source-composition change has yet been implemented.

### Latest DAG Runtime and Fleet Results

`dag-tests-after2` compiled without errors. The new canonical demo JSON oracle, snapshot DSL/pack round trips, and configuration/presence vectors passed before the full suite aborted during app initialization. The tool proof declared `dag-play` while the canonical controller is `s.dag.dag@1/*#editor`; a bare test registry also omitted migrated declarations. Corrected the controller used by proofs and action factories, declared the locale view action, and wired the test helper to the real registry.

Two isolated pre-existing runtime failures remain: disconnect inverse reorders edges and thus changes the content child identity; the command-envelope test applies through a store without the now-required mutation retirement factory. No full DAG test pass is claimed.

The fourth WASI fleet check reports 833 errors and 854 warnings across 59 selected plugin/extension packages. Mathematical module/registration edits and String printing fixes were made after that pass and need a fresh compile. All compilation targets remain unfinished.

### Explicit Aggregate Component Sources

Added `mutationAggregateSources` to the taxonomy authority. Six aggregate roots explicitly name their physical mutation collections: Mathematical, Note, FEM 2D and 3D, Sequence, and Drawing. The derive validates safe canonical roots, rejects empty/duplicate/malformed declarations and missing or symbolic-link sources, and retains per-leaf workspace, taxonomy, descriptor, physical source, semantic kind, and global wire-identity uniqueness checks. Explicit domain-operation components must contribute every registered operation. Sources are direct physical collections; declarations do not recursively inherit another aggregate’s authority.

The new language-neutral ten-case source fixture reproduced the missing component scope before implementation. After decoding quoted Rust string literals through Syn for the oracle comparison, all 15 derive tests passed (`aggregate-sources-after2`), including existing ownership, mandatory descriptor, and SHA-256 oracle tests. A complete WASI fleet compile is running to verify real aggregate const evaluation.

Files: `🧰️framework/🛍️products/💻️os/🔨️modules/🗣️dsl/✨️derive/🦀️.rs`, its `🧪️tests/🏛️mutation-aggregate-source-authority/🧫️fixtures/🧩️sources.json`, and `🧰️framework/🛍️products/🦑️repo/🔨️modules/📚️library/🔣️taxonomy.json`.

### Retained Command Paths and UTF-8 Progress Payloads

Qualified retained command types through their actual owning modules. Sequence progress translations now use UTF-8 string bytes instead of invalid non-ASCII byte literals. The Sequence mutation aggregate derives first-party tagged value codecs matching its Serde wire representation. VCS inspector uses the actual contract tree-row builder through a direct first-party dependency.

### Mathematical UI and Configuration

Updated graph, geometry, and table renderers to fallible contract nodes and component trees. Added scene-payload assertions. Replaced the empty custom presence runtime with the framework NoPresence channel; its independent schema declaration remains. Corrected remaining equation field/array codec calls. Configuration operations now use two source-owned mutation leaves, explicit JSON schemas/descriptors, and a four-case neutral oracle covering field preservation, inverse, text/binary codecs, and unchanged values. The unchanged-value outcome now retains the current configuration rather than applying a default whole-record snapshot. These new Mathematical tests have not yet run.

Updated configuration call sites:

- ✏️s/🔌️plugins/➗️mathematical/🗿️artifacts/➗️equation/🏅️standards/🔖️1/🪆️subsets/✳️any/✏️editor/🦀️.rs
- ✏️s/🔌️plugins/➗️mathematical/🗿️artifacts/➗️equation/🏅️standards/🔖️1/🪆️subsets/✳️any/✏️editor/🎚️config/🦀️.rs

- ✏️s/🔌️plugins/➗️mathematical/🗿️artifacts/➗️equation/🏅️standards/🔖️1/🪆️subsets/✳️any/✏️editor/🎮️commands/🗣️set-locale/🦀️.rs

### Forms Bounded JSON Calls

Scoped the existing Serde chunk oracle implementation to tests, implemented the missing first-party value conversion with the same 4,096-byte UTF-8 limit, and replaced runtime Serde JSON string calls with first-party codecs. Continuation arguments now serialize the declared SetTryValueStep payload directly. Corrected prefix iteration to use the existing content traversal. Added a seven-case language-neutral chunk fixture for ASCII, two-byte characters, four-byte characters, and a non-string rejection. This new Forms test is pending compilation; no pass is claimed.

Files: Forms `🎮️commands/🎯️set-try-value/🦀️.rs`, its `🧪️fixtures/🔣️chunks.json`, `🎮️commands/🗃️set-try-values/🦀️.rs`, and `🎚️config/🦀️.rs` under the any subset editor.
